Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in sr theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 383 and the invariant I(A,B) = 371 mod 3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on sr structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 383. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 371. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in sr theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 429 and the invariant I(A,B) = 378 mod 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 429 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the sr structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 378. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
